## Classification policy

- `pass`: a matching benchmark result CSV row was emitted for the matrix entry's problem size.
- `fail`: reserved for explicit per-size non-timeout failure markers; the staged artifacts used here did not include such markers.
- `timeout`: reserved for explicit per-size timeout markers; the staged artifacts used here did not include logs or per-size timeout markers.
- `no-result`: no matching benchmark result CSV row was present. Under the artifacts-only rule, missing rows remain `no-result` rather than being relabeled as fail or timeout from job-level failure alone.

Validation guard: `scripts/derive_mi300a_regular_finishability_evidence.py` rejects non-terminal snapshot metadata as current finishability input. With `benchmark-comparison/provenance/mi300a_problem_size_discovery_run_24959959195.json`, it exits nonzero because run `24959959195` has `status=queued` and `non_terminal_snapshot=true`.
